		icon = coal

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 2141
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation1
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 2
			owns_state = 2141
			controls_state = 2141
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 2141
			controls_state = 2141
			2141 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_2141_coal_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 57
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 2
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = coal
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			2141 = { set_state_flag = has_2141_templateResource_developed }
			2141 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = coal
					amount = 14
				}
			}
		}
	}

		icon = aluminium

		allowed = {

		}

		highlight_states = {
			highlight_state_targets = {
				state = 1143
			}
		}

		available = {
			has_tech = excavation3
			num_of_civilian_factories_available_for_projects > 3
			owns_state = 1143
			controls_state = 1143
		}

		visible = {
			owns_state = 1143
			controls_state = 1143
			1143 = {
				NOT = {
					has_state_flag = has_1143_aluminium_developed
				}
			}
		}

		fire_only_once = yes

		cost = 49
		days_remove = 60

		modifier = {
			civilian_factory_use = 3
		}

		ai_will_do = {
			base = 0.3
			modifier = {
				factor = 0
				AND = { #Too low MP we need to save PP for the laws
					has_manpower < 10000
					NOT = { has_idea = scraping_the_barrel }
				}
			}
			modifier = {  # Too low PP
				factor = 0.5
					has_political_power < 80
			}
			modifier = { #We have plenty of PP, avanti!
				factor = 3
				has_political_power > 200
			}
			modifier = { #Running out of the stuff, need more!
				factor = 5
				has_resources_in_country = {
					resource = aluminium
					amount < 1
				}
			}
		}
		remove_effect = {
			1143 = { set_state_flag = has_1143_templateResource_developed }
			1143 = {
				add_resource = {
					type = aluminium
					amount = 13
				}
			}
		}
